# Version 2 sweep record (`M17.E.1`, the stage-end sweep)

Generated by `tools/version_2_sweep.py`; do not edit. **Every number here is a measurement of a range, not a
verdict.** For each criterion below, the `[I]` constants its mechanism reads were drawn jointly over the ranges
declared in `config/sweep/sweep.md` and the criterion was run once per sample; *held* is the number of samples
whose verdict was PASS, of the samples that gave a verdict. The gating verdict is the one in the criterion's
ensemble record (*central verdict*); a fraction here changes no verdict and is never written as a pass or a
fail. A premise passing is never a finding (`M11.5`), and no premise is swept. A criterion shown as *declared,
not run* is listed under that heading in `config/sweep/sweep.md`: its ranges are declared, the cache holds no
result of it, and no number is shown for it. A criterion shown as *refused at the rerun* had a sample that
needed the rerun below while the criterion fixes fewer seeds of its own; the sweep refuses it at that sample
(`M17.A.1`), so it has no fraction, and the samples that ran up to that one are shown, not counted.

The sweep's rules (`config/sweep/sweep.md`, each `[I]`): `sweep_samples` 8 per criterion,
`sweep_seed` 2; the detection-limit rule (`M17.A.1`, plan D8): a sample whose first run left a
gating effect within 2.0 half-widths of zero was run again at a fixed
200 seeds and its verdict is the second run's (*reruns*). *Undetermined* samples did
not converge and do not count as holding; *errored* samples (the run raised, did not import or failed) are
counted here and left out of the denominator, never dropped.

`sweep_samples` 8 is the pilot's count (`docs/implementation_plan_version_2.md` D10), kept for the
full sweeps: one sample is an eighth of a fraction, so no band is drawn round it and no *narrow* mark is read,
which needs at least 32 samples (D14).

## The named ratio `tie_wear_to_transfer_ratio` (`M17.E.1`): where it entered

`tie_wear_to_transfer_ratio` = `tie_wear_rate` ÷ (`slow_tick_fast_ticks` × `calm_transfer_rate`), [I]: central value 0.01923, range 0.004808–0.07692 (log). The units differ: the numerator is a share per week (per slow tick), the denominator a share per contact, so the ratio is per week ÷ per contact. It entered the set of every swept criterion whose run is longer than `slow_tick_fast_ticks` (52) fast ticks, so that a slow tick fires before the run's last tick, and of no other (Q14); in each entered criterion every sample drew the ratio and `calm_transfer_rate` and derived `tie_wear_rate` from them. A run length is measured: seed 0 of each arm at the central constants, the longest arm's fast ticks. 26 of 34 criteria entered.

| Criterion | Run (fast ticks) | The ratio |
|---|---|---|

## `M11.C.48` — refused at the rerun (`M17.A.1`)

Sample 2 needs a rerun at 200 seeds, but the criterion fixes its own seed count at 100 (`config/bowen/criteria.md`), which the runner reads before the block and cap, so the sweep cannot rerun it at the declared count and refuses the criterion there (`M17.A.1`).
